*Over Saturation Is Only A Thing If...*

*You're playing on the same field as everyone else..* 

*Maybe you've grabbed the same website template.. Emails..* 

*A Carbon copy of the product creator & all the others pushing the same thing.. The exact same way.* 

*Now I get it.. "It's tried and true.. Why wouldn't I use it?" Right..* 

*Super early on this can be great to snag a few wins..* 

*But long term you'll want to remove yourself from the same field as everyone else..* 

*Simply by changing the positioning of what you're offering..* 

*You're promoting Product X..*

*What can you add on top of it to:* 

*👉Increase the perceived value because you're going the extra mile to give more..* 

*👉Priortize building your own ecosystem and pool of buyers (yess.. even if you're only an affiliate)* 

*👉Give you a new fresh coat of paint on the product you're promoting so not a single soul has the same thing as you..* 

*The last one is arguably one that you could implement & turn your sales notifications from a barren desert..* 

*To a parking lot in Walmart on a black-Friday sale..* 

*Something I've referred to as "The Always New Strategy"..* 

*Simply changing the position of how you're promoting your thing & will allow you to inject cash into your business each and every month with a tiny tweak..* 

*Like I did last year with a plethora of different products & offers. (Many of them are still Hot 🔥)*

*And like we've done this year with all of our product launches, which averaged out to \$1,000+ every day of 2022\.*

*Now, we've condensed this entire process to eliminating your competition & filling your future pipeline with more leads than you could shake a stick at Into 3 parts..* 

*And we dropped the entry price by 50% until 9/14..*

*So if you wanna plug into the exact same training our client used to pull in a quick \$6,000 with his own offer by making the same changes..*

*Don't Come From A Family Of Entrepreneurs?* 

*Me Either.. In all honesty.. Polar Opposite..* 

*My brother wears shirts like "Support Blue Collar" and takes pride in working for a big corporation..*   
*My dad drove semi trucks for 20+ years for a company delivering food to places you've likely ate at on the regular..*  
*My mom works as a peer counselor to people with substance abuse issues..*   
*I'd only ever worked in blue-collar jobs before investing into some training and getting in the rooms of other people doing what i wanted to be doing.*

*Hell I don't know if i'd ever even met a real entrepreneur before diving into the online world.* 

*And that is by far THE HARDEST thing about going all in.* 

*You're breaking down lessons that you've likely learned over a lifetime..* 

*Things that "aren't real or possible" for everyday people..* 

*Views and limiting beliefs of "How you have to make a living"*

*\$1,082 Every Single Day Of June..*  
*Even though this has been about the standard every day of 2022..*  
*This month was quite different..*  
*And instead of turning this into a piece of content about:*  
*"How you can accomplish this too with one easy payment of \$6,000 & we'll do it for you"*  
*I'll break down exactly how we did it..*  
*So you can be armed with the skills to actually do it yourself..*  
*Over and over again..*  
*Firstly.. Why was this month any different if we've cranked out numbers like this daily all year?*  
*👉We were building out a beginner-friendly course.. (something we can sell over and over again)*  
*✅Market Validation Stage*  
*We started doing market research, Looking for real-time feedback on what people were looking for.*  
*Inside our public groups.. This one \- Flip's Public Group*  
*Inside the Group Juice community \- And our 4-figure per month coaching program group.*  
*✅Build Out Training Structure & Docs..*  
*👉Since I'm a Google Fanboy \- We used the feedback we got from our audiences to put together a 5-day event.*  
*We packaged each day into a Google Doc & built out a pop-up FB group..*  
*✅Promo Mode*  
*Since we'd started teasing about a beginner-friendly training inside our content..*  
*👉We ran a live video with an invite..*  
*👉The following week content rotation became the problem we were solving inside of the 5-day event.. (Cycling through different things we were covering)*  
*✅We Sold 120 Spots In 3 Weeks At \$50 Per Person..*  
*Basically selling entry to a FB group where we would be hosting the live event..*  
*✅Ran All 5-days of the training..*  
*Everyone inside rocked with us for a week straight..*  
*✅Day 6..*  
*We extended a low-pressure invitation to rock with us every week for lifetime coaching calls..*  
*Opened it up for 15-spots to come to join us inside of Group Juice..*  
*Sold All 15\.*  
*✅Course Creation..*  
*We then took the videos we ran for the live event..*  
*Cut them up into digestible chunks..*  
*And uploaded them into a course..*  
*Where we doubled the price to \$100 & added some other bonuses inside..*  
*Wrapping up our little "Launch"*  
*With \$31,400 cash collected for June..*  
*But more importantly an Evergreen Asset ( A Course)*  
*We can sell.. Over and over again..*  
*Now, you can do this as a complete beginner..*  
*Even if you don't have a product of your own..*  
*And I know because I've done this dozen of times with affiliate products..*  
*Here's How:*  
*✅Take the program you're promoting & look at where it's lacking.. Ask yourself.. "What would make this better?"*  
*✅Prep your own training (Don't record them just yet \- Just put together resources to do them)*  
*✅Roll Out Your Offer \- Start talking about how you're going to be doing a training on X for the next 4 weeks (Weekly calls covering the training you just prepped when someone purchases the product from step 1\)*  
*✅Run The Training inside a pop-up group & record them..*  
*You know have an asset that will:*  
*1️⃣Allow you to promote the same exact product as everyone else from a completely different angle (because you can promote your 4-step process instead & still sell the same thing) \- It will be new & shiny just like everyone likes..*  
*2️⃣You now have an asset that you can bolt onto any affiliate product that your training was relevant to.. That is unique to you & your brand.. Will also allow you to step away \- Giving you the ability to provide more value to your buyers without trading your direct time for it.. And if you do the offer just became even better..*  
*3️⃣You now just got paid to create something.. That you can use to fuel your business.. Whether you're leveraging it to put on top of an affiliate product or you're using it to sell as an entry-level product to identify buyers for your premium prices products..*  
*A tad bit of an advanced strategy..*  
*But what I will tell you \- If you start looking at things through the lens of building out a catalog of assets & ways to actually structure out your brand & business..*  
*It will put you miles ahead of the vast majority..*  
*If you're looking to dip your toes into the world of online biz..*

*Your bullshit radar going off every time you see somebody talking about making money online?*  
*I Get it I really do.*  
*Just A few years back I was late night scrolling through social media trying to escape the fact that I had to be at work in 3 hours.*

*Side note.. The drop shipping expert turned and NFT expert kickstarted My journey to going down a rabbit hole of finding the best business model..*  
*And without boring you by having to read a 2,000 page Facebook post on your Sunday afternoon I'll summarize.*  
*Over The next couple of months I bought into three different high ticket programs all teaching me about different business models hoping The next one would be easier.*  
*And over the next couple years it was a lot of trial and error finding out what worked and didn't work and what I actually wanted to be doing with my business.*  
*Coming off of a fuck ton of failure I discovered the power of high ticket affiliate marketing.*  
*And No I'm not going to preach to you that it's the coolest business model since sliced bread but what I can say is this is where my business changed.*  
*To Help you avoid some of the BS that I had to go through let me break down what actually changed in my business.*  
*Which can be applied to whatever you're selling or whatever business model you are leveraging.*  
*✅The content probably one of the most slept on aspects of building and having success with any online business*  
*The beauty of creating content on a regular basis is that it works for you at scale and what I mean by that is once you've put a piece of content out into the world*  
*It It has the ability to build a relationship with your ideal clients 24/7*  
*The number of people and conversations that you'll start generating off of the back end of your content is insane especially when you're doing it for prolonged periods of time*  
*Not To mention how easy the sales become when your prospect has consumed a ton of your content.*  
*✅ Your offers. Like what you're selling you know?*  
*But More importantly why someone would even be buying that product in the first place..*  
*This is one of the biggest shifts you can make in your sales process by conveying what your clients will get from buying instead of the product itself*  
*People aren't buying group juice because it's a 4-day workshop.*  
*They are buying it because they're stuck in a cycle of adding friends and sending cold DMs and are looking for a way to get out of it.*  
*They are buying it because they see the power of having a Facebook group and want to learn how to rapidly grow and monetize a community of their own.*  
*The solution is why people buy products. Where is going to take them.*  
*Example: What do you think sounds more appealing?*  
*🅰️ I can show you how to have a Facebook group of your own.*  
*🅱️ I can show you how to have 200 leads banging at your door in the next 7 days.*  
*Let me know Your answer in the comments 👇*  
*✅ And lastly one of them most important pillars. Selling.*  
*Now I'm not saying you have to be some sleazy salesman knocking on people's doors trying to get them to buy that fancy knife set.*

# *October 2022 Recap..*

*This year between ourselves and our client's businesses we're pushing \$600,000+ sold online..*  
*Without:*  
*❌ Having to play into the whole \- Quick & Easy Financial freedom, Copy and paste your way to 100k online*  
*❌Participating in the digital circle jerk.. Where you'll only help someone have success if they're selling your product..*  
*❌Having to get blessed by an algorithm.. Or regularly go viral to make sales..*  
*❌Sluffing off prospects to a closer who likely doesn't see anything in your prospect beyond a dollar sign..*  
*❌Gatekeep strategies to get them to work with us even closer.. 99% of our coaching clients have reached out asking to how they could sign up to pay us 4-figures per month.*  
*❌Charing an arm & leg to give people lifetime support & proven strategies that will help them actually build a brand of their own online. (countless people have told us we should be charging more)*  
*❌Twisting people's arms to work with us & buy our stuff.. The majority of them come to us & stay once they're rockin' with us..*  
*And for the select few that do leave..*  
*It's because we're not everyone's cup of tea..*  
*We cuss.. We preach Real Work.. Playing Longer Term..*  
*Looking at things in years instead of days..*  
*Now that's not to say you can't make money jumping onto a quick cash grab \- or a plug-and-play type thing..*  
*Where you'll pump traffic to something & they'll sell for you.*  
*Hell, you could probably make a decent chunk of change relatively quickly with them..*  
*But here's where the problem is in my opinion..*  
*What's After?*

* *Are you building your own pool of buyers or theirs?*  
* *Did you learn how to close the sales yourself \- so you could potentially delegate that task when you do grow?*  
*   
* *Where is the ascension? Do you have to regularly pump fresh blood to make an income or is there an opportunity to increase the lifetime value of each person coming in?*  
*   
* *What about when "that offer" is no longer a thing.. (for those that are fairly new to the online world \- products come and go extremely quickly)*  
* 

*Now, don't get me wrong, I used to do the opposite of what i do now..*  
*Hell, my first viral video was about selling your dirty socks on the internet..*  
*But in light of learning & understanding what type of brand I wanted to build (which took me years by the way)*  
*All of the content & messaging changed..*  
*Preaching about easy money doesn't do a good job of getting long-term clients, because once they find out it's a tad harder than promised..*  
*They're usually off to the next best thing..*  
*We want to work closely with people for years..*  
*☑️Being a part of those huge impacts you're actually having on people's lives..*  
*☑️Helping them actually understand how it all works & helping them deploy it.. so they have the skills to make money online indefinitely..*  
*A tad bit of a rant, but an important one..*  
*Zooming out a bit & really figuring out what it is you're looking to accomplish online..*  
*And hey if that's the quickest & easiest cash grab, more power to you, but I'm definitely not your guy to help you do that..*  
*But if having a brand of your own & an ecosystem of your own is something you'd like to accomplish..*  
*So you can set yourself free for years to come because you understand how the entire process works & know how to leverage parts that you enjoy while eliminating the ones you don't..*  
*Then I might be able to help you out :P*  
*We're down to about 8-days left before our offer that could do just that is going up by couple of grand..*  
*Pop me a message here if you'd like to chat about it 👉*  
*Have a kick-ass weekend*  
*Nate Ryan*

Something I Wish I Would Have Understood Sooner..  
The power building your own assets..

☑️But building a catalog of your own assets..  
Will give you full scope..  
To make more money..  
And meet your audience where they're at..  
Now, these don't need to be anything crazy..  
👉 Sell An Affiliate Product \- Run a 4-week pop-up group and provide your own training on the back end..

1. You now have an asset you can sell as a stand-alone product to ascend people into your premium price products..  
2. You have an evergreen asset of your own that you can bolt onto your affiliate product..  
3. You now have a completely different angle to promote from.. Your "asset" can become the face of your promo & your collecting the cash through the sale of affiliate products..

👉Run a back-end support group / Coaching calls?  
Over a period of time.. You'll build up a catalog of content you can indeed sell..  
Individually.. As a whole.. Or package them into a catered training..  
Similar to how Flip & myself rolled out the Group Juice Core Accelerator..  
We probably already had 50 Hours of coaching calls inside the back office before we even hosted our first joint call..  
Heck. We've sold stand-alone videos from inside for low-medium ticket prices..

You sell someone a \$100 training.. And they achieve quick-fire results out of it..  
The chances of you ascending them into your 1k, 2k, Or even 5k offer becomes a breeze..  
Very low pressure & simple sales process..  
That is the ultimate beauty of this online world..  
All this leveraged content you're creating.. You can in fact sell :D

*"I Just Don't Know What To Post"*  
☝One of the most common things I hear..  
So let's Dive In..  
A couple of things first \-  
✅Know the objective of the piece of content you're putting out.. (more on that later)  
✅Gotta Know What Field Your Playing In \- What space you're building your brand inside of. (can go micro or broader)  
Long-Form Content Framework \- The same one i use for just about every post  
👉The Headline \- Hook \- Bait

* This is what is going to pull your reader in & get them to consume your content..  
* Do your best to keep your hook hyper-relevant to the piece of content you're planning on creating..

For example \- the hook of this post is: *"I Just Don't Know What To Post"*  
Why?  
🅰 Because i know it's something many actively struggle with  
🅱This entire post is a solution to that problem  
👉Lead Into A Problem

* A mutual frustration  
* Something you know people are struggling with  
* Something you've struggled with  
* What is in the frustrated state actually feels like

Again 😛 This entire piece of content is about an overarching problem people are facing  
👉Backstory

* Real experience  
* Anchoring this the section above \- the problem that was happening & your story tied to it  
* Nailing this section will make the future ones easier as well

👉The Solution

* How do you get out of the problem  
* How do you overcome that mutual frustration  
* Leading into the solution that you've found for the problem (Aka \- the product you're selling)

👉Proof Of Concept

* The results that have come out of doing X or implementing the solution  
* Growth \- Breakthroughs  
* Wins \- Monetary and non-monetary

👉The Call To Action

* what do you want the people who're consuming your content to do  
* For pure engagement or to generate potential sales convos

Real-Time Example? You Got It 👇  
*Just Dont Know What To Post?*  
*I know the feeling..*  
*A Couple of years back when i dived into this content creation world..*

*Just to get home and hit a wall as soon as I sat down at my computer..*

*To writing a post on the fly all about the struggles of content creation..*

*And if it wasn't perfect i posted it anyway..*  
*Real-time example \- Posted about 30 Tiktok videos and didn't even realize the picture was fuzzy AF \- probably need to get my eyes checked..*  
*Putting less stress on myself about creating a masterpiece & prioritizing just sharing me..*

*A Couple of reasons why \-*

*10 Years Salary In 165 Days.. 🤯*  
Back when I got sober..  
I snagged a job at a company called RailPro..  
Where we'd install custom aluminum guard rails on the balconies all around Downtown Portland Oregon..  
Aka \- I'd spend all day outside in the pouring down rain leaning over the edge of a 10th-story balcony to attach a railing to it..  
It didn't light my soul on fire by any means.. In reality, it probably did the opposite..  
But freshly getting my life together it's the only thing "I knew how to do"  
I wrapped up that entire year with \$21,995 in wages earned for 2018..  
A pretty radical difference from where I am today..  
Our slowest month all year was just shy of that at 18.1k..  
Hence the title.. Fast forward 4 years..  
We've pulled in 10 years of my salary that I used to make in a blue-collar job since January 28th..  
*And why the fuck does this matter to you?*  
Because mid-last year I had a huge aha moment..  
A breakthrough.. Whereas prior I was seriously struggling to get any results really..  
It took me a couple of years to hit my first 5-figure month online..  
And here's the biggest lesson I've learned through it all..  
Keep it simple af..  
At its core.. This entire game is basic..  
And most overcomplicate the fuck out of it all..  
Create content that connects with your audience \- doesn't matter what it is.. Can literally be you making a cup of coffee and talking..  
Or a long piece of content like such.. To help someone achieve a goal..  
Connect with the people who consume your content..  
Have a chat with them..  
Plant a seed.. Talk to them like a human..  
And eventually, make an offer to them..  
90% of the people I'm selling to today.. are people that have been seeing my stuff for a while..  
We've chatted previously.. We have a connection already established..  
Whether that's through my leveraged stuff like my content..  
Or a 1-to-1 scenario through the DM..  
Now I know this might seem tangential..  
But it's relevant.. Everyone wants speed..  
*Money As Fast As Possible..*  
And when you operate like this it usually does the opposite of making sales..  
It repels people because you have that sales breath...  
Sound like that dude at the Christmas party trying to get everyone to come over and watch them do some knife presentation..  
Build your future pipeline.. A pool of people that will buy from you eventually..  
This will be different for each person who comes into your world...  
I've had people buy a \$3,000+ product a few days after coming into the community & I've had people take as long as a year..  
And what fuels both.. Is proactively showing up in your business & posting :P  
Now I wish I could give you some miracle growth for your online business..  
But the key to doing 10 years salary within 165 days..  
Is prioritizing relationships over revenue..  
Because the relationships are what generate the revenue..  
And all the "Non-monetary" things you're doing inside of your business..  
Is what produces the monetization..  
Happy Tuesday ya'll..  
If you'd like some help rolling out our stupid simple framework that'll have you producing cash through your content quick..  
I'm only a message away..  
Cheers,  
Nate Ryan

*How We Sold 145 Spots For Our Virtual Event In 18 Days..*  
*Mind you \- We ran this entire event live \- so there wasn't time spent creating a product that didn't sell..*  
*Let's dive in..*  
*👉Utlizied actual feedback from people to figure out what we wanted to cover..*  
*Started asking questions throughout a few of our groups.. To clients..*  
*"If you were at square one, what would you like to know about building an online business of your own"*  
*(This was the fuel for everything we decided to cover)*  
*👉Built out a quick pop-up Facebook Group*  
*Where we're hosting the live training \- recording them and then turning them into an evergreen training we can sell over and over again..*  
*(The art of getting paid to create something)*  
*👉Went full-send on talking about it..*  
*Every piece of content for a couple of weeks was about our upcoming virtual event..*  
*Not the "event as a whole" but breaking down different sub-sections of what would be included..*  
*What participating in the event would do for the people who take it..*  
*(Putting a deadline can really light a fire under people to jump in before it closes)*  
*Not bad for a couple of weeks promo..*  
*145 people at \$50 \- Quick 7k ish pulled in within 17 days..*  
*Some of them might jump into our full-fledge program Group Juice At \$1,000*  
*And a select few might come over into our \$5,000 \- 6 month coaching offer..*  
*Do they have to? No..*  
*In reality \- each step isn't the perfect match for everyone..*  
*But that's the real power of building out assets online \- and meeting your prospects where they're at..*  
*Because turning a client who had an amazing experience with you for \$50..*  
*Does have a very real possibility of turning into a high-paying client.*  
*Now i know this is a bit of advanced strategy.. and something that wasn't even on my radar for my first year in the online space..*  
*And the cool part is you can do this with or without creating the products yourself.*  
*Can do this off the back-end of affiliate products..*  
*And it will usually cause a fuck ton of curiosity because of simply changing the way you're selling something*  
*Hope ya'll are having a killer week..*  
*Cheers*  
*Nate Ryan*  
*Dropped some quick feedback we had from today alone :P*

*Let's Break A Limiting Belief..*

* *You need a huge number of people to make a lot of money..*  
* *You need viral traffic..*

*Unless you're cranking out 7,000 leads per day with the hottest strategy your business is shit..*  
*In every video I've put out in the last week has less than 1,000 views..*  
*There are multiple posts I've put out.. With ZERO engagement..*  
*Yet, we've managed to collect \$17,000 in the last 7 days...*  
*And better yet..*  
*We've sold products to people who're actually a good fit..*  
*To buy.. To start an online business..*  
*Also meaning.. The chances of them buying something from us again is very likely..*  
*This is the real power of being Crystal clear in your content..*  
*About: Who your product is for.. What problem it solves.. The work that will be required to actually have success online..*  
*It makes your sales process extremely short.. All the sales we've done this week..*  
*Were without discovery calls or zoom at all..*  
*Usually just a quick chat back and forth & sending over a 10-page Google doc (because we're using content to do selling for us)*  
*And yes.. This means you're more than likely going to be getting less Volume..*  
*But idk about you.. But if you haven't gone viral before \- it's overrated AF..*  
*And will likely be spending all day responding to people who're just throwing around the idea of doing something..*  
*VS targeted messages through your content.. So those chats you do have with people are more inclined to actually buy your premium-priced products because they know what they're getting into.*

*Cracked 10k Monthy Recurring Revenue..*  
*Meaning.. Starting Each & Every Month With 5-Figures Hitting The Bank Account..*  
*With the goal of taking it to \$20,000 by the end of the year..*  
*Quite the contrast to my first inside sobriety when I pulled in a whopping \$21,000 for an entire year working in construction..*  
*So How?*  
*👉Pre-Full time entrepreneur online..*  
*I cut as many costs as I could.. So I could invest in myself.*  
*Got me in the rooms of people who were doing the exact things that I wanted to be doing.*  
*50% of every single paycheck I made was put into a separate bank account \- which was then used to level up my skillset.*  
*Because I knew if I could acquire skills that I didn't have & people wanted to learn..*  
*I could sell them..*  
*(You'd be surprised how much you could find if you looked at cutting out the tiny things you purchase daily \- Skip that morning \$3 Muffin & over the span of 12 months you'll have extra 4-figures in your bank)*  
*👉Post Full-Time Online..*  
*Built Proof Of Concept & Credibility..*  
*\$100,000 In sales my first year being full-time & launched a product of our own coming into 2022..*  
*Revealing everything that caused myself & clients to have their breakthroughs..*  
*(A big one \- When we brought a product to market \- we knew it was working \- Because we'd both been using it for over a year & our clients were already having success with it)*  
*Shortly after Flip moss & myself rolled out our High Ticket Continuity program..*  
*4-figure investment per month to lock arms with us & have hand's on help in doing more cool shit..*  
*Like launching your own signature offers.. Or smashing the 5-figure month mark with affiliate products..*  
*So in turn \- all the layers we've created inside our business are to identify those who would be a good fit to lock arms with us long term..*  
*Now, Don't get me wrong..*  
*I'm saying anyone & everyone should go out there and roll out their coaching offer like 90% of the overnight experts you're seeing right now..*

*Your Content Makes Me Want To Pull Out My Wallet..*  
*And Buy... Even Though I've Already Bought 🤯*  
*This is a message I got yesterday from someone who's been consuming some of my content.*  
*👉Can you imagine how seamless sales become when you have a dialed-in content strategy?*  
*I'll Pain The Picture for you..*  
*👉\$50-5,000 sales within a few messages back and forth..*  
*❌No Strategy Sessions Or Clarity Calls..*  
*❌No twisting people's arms or preying on some false promises of riches overnight..*  
*☑️Real Relationships Being Established Through The Content..*  
*That:*  
*👉Attracts your ideal clients.. Those who're gonna actually show up & put in the work to build their online business..*  
*👉Repells those who aren't a good fit to dive into the online world.. Let alone buy premium prices products.*  
*So the real question..*  
*How do you do this effectively?*  
*First \- Stop trying to be a carbon copy of everyone else..*  
*It's all over your feeds on a few different platforms..*  
*The Same "Template For FB Posts" Leveraging someone else's screenshots as your own..*  
*☑️Embed YOU Into your content.. I will regularly throw inappropriate jokes in because.. Well, that's my personality..*  
*And diving into a personal brand \- without having a personal brand is like being on Keto but only eating doughnuts..*  
*Dont feel like you have a personal brand?*  
*If you're selling products as an affiliate \- Or creating content in general..*  
*At it's core \- you're selling you.. The product is just the monetization..*  
*☑️Go Pain & Solution Focused..*  
*People purchase things because of a feeling..*  
*To escape something.. Or achieve something..*  
*There is an emotion behind the reason for "Buying anything"*  
*Focusing more on providing solutions to problems inside your content or digging into problems & presenting your "Product" as the solution is the key.*  
*A Perfect Example \- A lot of content is all over my page right now..*  
*"How you can get Thousands to amount of FREE LEADS"*  
*👆It's presenting a solution to a problem most feel like they're facing in their business.. Leads..*  
*(Would argue that you don't need more leads.. You need better leads.. But that's just me :P)*  
*Why is this effective?*  
*Because the conversations you're generating off of the "Content" are of higher quality..*  
*They know why they're there..*  
*Like I've said a dozen times..*  
*And will likely keep saying it because I like beating off a dead horse..*  
*Long Content \- Short Sales Process..*  
*Short Content \- Long Sales Process.*  
*The people who come off your longer content (will usually be fewer) Are problem & Solution Aware because you've established it.*  
*Whereas those coming off your extremely short content are there for a different reason.. A lead magnet.. A free resource..*  
*Both can work just as effectively.. But you will need to pick up the slack in one or the other.*  
*Whether that's at scale through your content \- Or through a 1-to-1 scenario in a dm or a call..*  
*Your Content Has the ability to basically clone yourself..*  
*And build rapport to the masses..*  
*This is why I'll always put a heavier focus on creating better content..*  
*As you should too :P*  
*Cheers,*  
*Nate Ryan*

*Digital Dickhead.. Ever Been One?*  
*Imagine you've encountered plenty..*  
*I know I was one for a hot minute..*  
*Hungry for sales.. And jumping down people's throats to slam them with an offer if they showed the slightest interest in starting an online business..*  
*Ironically.. Operating this way actually did the polar opposite of what I was hoping to achieve..*  
*Sales breath so bad as if I'd just chomped a couple of cloves of garlic..*  
*I remember hearing it from JC somewhere about detaching from the sales..*  
*It took me a really long time to actually understand & position myself this way inside of my business..*  
*Now, it's easier said than done..*  
*When you're attempting to escape a job you absolutely hate or maybe you've invested your last dollar into a program that promised huge returns & you're literally banking on those sales to survive..*  
*(Also why I'm big on actually talking about what it takes to cross 100k.. Because selling stuff to those who aren't in a good spot to implement let alone invest is a recipe for disaster)*  
*And if there is one lesson that I've learned over the years and something that I really wish I would have understood sooner..*  
*Is the Customer Journey..*  
*Very rarely are those who see a piece of your content..*  
*Have that first initial conversation with..*  
*Or just become aware of you as a brand owner are going to be ready to buy..*  
*It's usually going to take multiple touch points..*  
*Continued consumption of your content or conversing..*  
*Moving them along towards the sale..*  
*Most would be blown away by the number of times I've heard..*  
*"I don't have money to invest"*  
*Just to end up jumping in at a later date and at a high price point..*  
*The leveraged content & the continued rapport building..*  
*This is why Flip moss & myself are always preaching about:*  
*Giving yourself more time..*  
*In your promos & inside your business in general..*  
*Different pieces of content you put out & the angles you're leveraging will connect with people in different ways..*  
*Ie- you could have said something yesterday that didn't strike an accord with person X..*  
*But the piece of content you put out tomorrow could..*  
*Now, arguably content is one aspect that most overthink.. And seriously struggle with..*  
*And if you want the exact roadmap we've used & content framework to turn our social media into a multi-6 figure business..*  
*Then you need to snag our Triple M.. Not only will we be arming you with Content Strategy..*  
*But a crystal clear process on how to actually monetize it..*

*That's what you do.. "How hard you work to provide for your family.. Is what makes the man"*

*The root of me "wheeling & dealing"..*

*"How The Fuck Have You Done This For 8 Months?"*  
*Is a question we had from someone when we were on a call with our GJCA clients..*  
*The Topic what nurturing content..*  
*How [Flip Moss](https://www.facebook.com/FlipMoss88?__cft__%5B0%5D=AZU67l_HEsVMhCPVsSZKuIBgl7kVB6BHVEWsOchvuPCew7OXnHpHYWapKhSMiAlJP7ohOnTFF45Pq_HX2NgpiTt0u0aTuFd2QwGIrRGss3GK4H1x5opbTkssaEv6_W0vHtg&__tn__=-%5DK-R) & Myself are regularly utilizing Free content creation..*  
*To \- Build Rapport \- Establish a relationship and sell products in unison..*  
*Key thing... Selling something..*  
*But without giving people the douchebumps.. coming across as needy.. Twisting people's arms to buy our shit..*  
*Or bait every Joe blow to bust out their wallet because we made some overhyped promise..*  
*And the analogy we ended up using was imagining your audiences in buckets filling up with water..*  
*(Killer picture below Flip drew on the fly on our call.. Don't think i could do that myself lol)*  
*The "goodwill" you build up with your audience.. (The water)*  
*Is filled up when you provide..*  
*You shed light on the processes you're doing..*  
*Experiences.. Lifestyle.. You're sharing you..*  
*And when you make offers (sell something publicly through the content)*  
*It depletes that bucket..*  
*Also why you'll see many who're constantly blasting.*  
*Buy My Shit.. Buy My Shit.. And not picking any momentum..*  
*They're attempting to build a pool of sales out of an empty bucket..*  
*No rapport was built..*  
*So how we're doing this in unison..*  
*Just like we did over the last couple of week with our Micro promo for PPT \- Something like \$40,000 collected in 14 days..*  
*The content being created is meant to do a few things at the same time..*  
*Provide value.. Actually shed light on the processes we're doing in our business.. How it's working & how people can actually do it themselves..*  
*Plant seeds.. For future clients.. (Yes it was 1000% ok if the people getting value from the content don't buy for another 90 days.. That's how it usually works anyways )*  
*Make Offers.. We're openly making offers through the content.. And giving people the option to either come join us or not..*  
*We're continuously filling both buckets at the same time..*  
*Now, if you want a real time example of what this type of content looks like..*

*The 1-to-1 Rule..*  
*For each step, you take into personal development..*  
*You NEED to match it with a step of implementation..*  
*Let's imagine your left leg is learning something..*  
*And your right leg is Application..*  
*Firstly why is this important?*  
*👉 If you don't consistently match each step.. You'll go in circles..*  
*`⭕Consume More Information. Consume More Information. Consume More Information.`*  
*This is how you'll end up with a google drive full of lead magnets..*  
*A favorites bar bookmarked with online courses you've purchased..*  
*A vast amount of "knowledge" without actually having applied 99% of them..*  
*(I did this for about a year.. Bought more and more programs & never applied.. Constantly look for the next golden ticket)*  
*Oh, I know everything about online business??*  
*`⭕Application. Application. Application.`*  
*This is how you'll end up being consistent in your business with minimal to zero results to show for it.*  
*It really hurts my heart when I've been seeing people posting daily for the last 12 months..*  
*And they're doing the same thing they were when they started..*  
*"Yet it still hasn't started working"*  
*If it's broken.. Fix it..*  
*(This is how I ended up with a massive blog & youtube channel full of content nobody was looking at.. I posted to "be consistent")*  
*✅Learn.*  
*✅Apply*  
*✅Learn.*  
*✅Apply*  
*✅Learn.*  
*✅Apply*  
*And that's not to say \- everything lesson comes from buying a new program, investing in a new thing, or snagging a new lead magnet..*  
*Everything you're doing in your business will yield a lesson \- which then gives you a new position to apply..*  
*A Failure. A Mistake. A Breakthrough. Something Works.*  
*"New Levels, New Devils"*  
*That is the nature of this entire world..*  
*You're testing things..*  
*Optimizing things..*  
*And the lesson I would want you to yield from this piece of content..*  
*👉If you're stuck in consuming more content & are constantly seeking tips & tricks.. Apply something.. Like now.. More & more information isn't going to get you closer to the goal if you don't start implementing what you're learning..*  
*👉If you're constantly applying & things aren't working.. Change it up.. Very rarely will it "just start working" and if it does \- it won't become repeatable.*  
*All I got for you today \-*  
*Get out there & kick some ass..*  
*You deserve it.*  
*Nate Ryan*

*What Most Won't Tell You About Starting An Online Business..*  
*There is no one set way to pave your way to digital dollars..*  
*The number of strategies available at your disposal is about as complex as those skin-covered creatures called humans..*  
*You're going to want to lean into something you can & want to be doing..*  
*Now let me elaborate.*  
*Because this isn't something that really clicked until I was years into the online game..*  
*You see.. A lot of the people who're having higher levels of success in this online world are all leveraging a process slightly unique to themselves..*  
*👉Pumping mass volume through their content \- Focusing on getting as many leads as they can so they can convert them on the back end into sales*  
*👉Leveraging very targeted strategies \- which gets A LOT Less volume \- but a higher conversion rate on the back end*  
*👉Outsourcing \- Having a team of closers & VA's doing outreach & booking calls.*  
*👉Taking sales calls..*  
*👉Not doing any sales calls.*  
*And I mention it primarily because when I started..*  
*I used to think that everything was one size fits all..*  
*You see someone else doing something.. And you have to follow their plan exactly..*  
*Or you see someone else doing another thing & think you're doing something wrong..*  
*It's your business.. Your unique ecosystem..*  
*And I would encourage anyone to find a process that they enjoy..*  
*That aligns with what they want to be doing long term..*  
*For me..*  
*When I got clear on what type of business I actually wanted big picture \- Things became fun & I made a whole lot more money..*  
*As a whole..*  
*Do you know what you actually want your business to look like?*  
*Let me know in the comments \-*  
*I Dropped mine 😎*

*Perfection Paradigm..*  
*You know that crippling feeling that everything needs to be "all lined up" before you start..*

*We spent all week over in VA with our Group Juice Core Accelerator Clients..*

*Imperfect Action.. Flip moss & myself are always talking about this..*

* *Demi Thomas ended up getting stuck for 8+ hours at the airport.. So we ran a couple of live pieces of training from the car..*  
*   
* *We got our live event all set up.. Just to realize you couldn't even see the projector or any of the "Fancy" stuff.. So we used a dusty computer monitor that Flip had laying around at his house..*  
*   
* *There we 5 digital marketers inside a house with super spotty internet.. So we ended up running the entire 6 hours mastermind on a phone's hotspot that I bought in 2017..*  
* *Spilled a can of seltzer water all over my shirt about 30 seconds before i was up to start speaking..*  
* *I ended up recording the entire mastermind in the wrong format.. So Video editing with zooming in and out were aplenty..*  
* *We kicked off the PPT Call with Jamie G \- The lighting was terrible so it looked like Flip & myself were streaming from the closet..*

*The feedback we had was absolutely insane..*  
*Something like 40+ people jammed with us all day long..*  
*Jill Anne & Leanna drove out and jammed with us all day..*  
*And in light of all the "imperfections"*

*Schedule was a bit different this last week, I was more quiet than usual, mainly because I was putting in 12+ hour days on this unit.*  
*We got this much done in 4 days, only thing left is shingles, siding and figuring out how we wanna finish the ceiling underneath.*  
*I ain't gunna lie either, I'm sore as hell and took today to recover.*  
*Still aint 100% haha.*  
*Brought back how I use to feel when I was working 7 days straight & 60 to 90 hours a week.*  
*(Mad respect for those still working like that)*  
*But I'm also here to tell ya that ya don't have to grind that hard.*  
*Not in today's time.*  
*I worked like that because that was all I knew and how I was raised to work.*  
*As a man I really thought that's what we were suppose to do.*  
*I use to brag about 40 hrs overtime*   
*I worked like that until I knew better*  
*Enough about me, let's talk about you.*  
*You likely have folks in your life that want what's best for you while also casting judgment on your 'crazy ideas' or your 'big vision'*  
*And that's because they want what's best for you based on what they know and/or based on what they werent able to do*  
*A quote I recently read in a book, 'be your future self now' hit hard and stuck with me*  
*"Don't take advice from someone you wouldn't trade places with"*  
*For me, I had plenty of folks I knew I DID NOT want to model or take advice from.*  
*You probably do too*  
*You've got to draw a line in the sand for YOURSELF and choose the path you want to go that will lead to life ya want.*  
*Mine was hard, still is hard Butttttttt, I wouldn't change it for nothing.*  
*I'm glad I chose the hard because there weren't many people on that road less traveled.*  
*3 years I failed and fell flat on my face with bad business models and tons of money invested into lessons that have molded me into what you see today.*  
*I am still bothered by judgement of others, but ya know what?*  
*I still operate and show up how I want to, speaking on the things I want to, and most importantly....*  
*Living life how I want to with my wife & kids*  
*I don't want to be like the masses for I am not like the masses*  
*I'm am my own person that has different values than others*  
*You success may be defined differently than others and that's perfectly ok\!*  
*Own it.*  
*Stop putting off what you really wanna do and start being the person you really wanna be*  
*Act accordingly*   
*I used to make decisions based on where I was in life at the moment*  
*Now I make decisions based on where I want to go in life now and later*  
*You've got to set big goals and then give yourself a longer runway to hit those goals*  
*I want my girls to be straight and also be able to tell their daddy's story*  
*So figure out WHY you want to be successful as an entrepreneur and hold that reason super tight*  
*Then do whatever you have to so you can surround yourself with folks thst ha e done the very things*   
*Flip is signing  off*

*What kind of lifestyle do you want to build?*  
*For me its quite simple and it hasnt taken me that long to get to where I am so I wanna walk ya through how my day starts*  
*First, we are live breaking down all the GJ 2.0 updates at 11am est and how it affects you whether you’re in already or still on the fence*  
*My morning: I wake up and head to the kitchen to make coffee*  
*The wife and I split the tasks to get the girls ready for school*  
*Water bottles made, book bags, lunches made and chocolate milk*  
*I let the fathead pitties out and go feed the chickens*  
*Then I take the girls to school and we jam on the way*  
*The ride back I catch up on a training, podcast or audible and they are all in reference to personal development or business in some way*  
*It sets the tone for the day*  
*Once home the wife is at the gym usually and I get to work*  
*This morning I fell up the steps and luckily the ring doorbell didnt catch it lol, so I had to make new coffee*  
*I then grabbed the laptop and hit the back deck and I jump into*  
*Catching up with core clients*  
*Checking emails*  
*FB dms*  
*Check sales*  
*Plan some content, get it recorded or written*  
*This gives me time to knock out most of my non negotiables before the rest of the day*  
*Tuesday, Wednesday and Thursday are our ‘Busy days’*  
*We have GJ weekly call on Tuesdays and Private Client Core calls on Thursdays, both at 11 am*

*This what we’ve done to do over \$100,000 already in 2023 only working about 10-15 hours a week in 8 steps.*  
*This will grow your personal brand, build your audience, gain influence and make you a full time income to the tune of Multi 6 figures without fancy tech, sales calls or reposting others viral content via copy & paste and hoping the shit works*  
*1️⃣ Ya gotta Define Your Brand and what's important to YOU*   
     *\- What do you value the most?*   
     *\- What interests you in life and biz?*   
     *\- What are you aiming for as far as goals, wants and desires?*   
     *\- What is your mission for yourself and others?*   
     *\- What are your standards, perspectives and morals?*   
     *\- What is your story, go back far as ya want*  
     *\-  What is your plan to help others look like ?*   
*2️⃣ Be sure to Consistently Show Up On your social media platforms, even if its just one to start*  
     *\- Create written content & videos about everything from step 1*  
     *\- At a minimum get at least 1 piece of content posted every day*   
     *\- Don't worry about fancy ass formats or video edits, just get it done*  
     *\- Pro Tip: Your first few posts and videos will likely suck, post em anyway*  
*3️⃣ Be yo mother loving selfffffff, Authentic, real & raw*  
      *\- Your first goal is not to go viral or get mad engagement, it’s to find your voice and style that fits you*   
      *\- View count, likes & engagement are metrics that DO NOT define you, dont focus on that*  
      *\- You want to find your people and being yourself following step one is how ya do that*   
*4️⃣ Your Hook & Headline is the bees knees (make it good & get to the point)*  
      *\- This should let them know what its about and why they need to watch or read*  
      *\- The first 3 seconds of any video or written post*    
         *Must grab and hold their attention*   
      *\- Even the 2nd line (sub hook/headline)*  
      *\- Surprise them, cliff hang em, shock em, be controversial at times*  
      *\- Make it clear who the post/video is for quickly*  
*5️⃣ Your video or post should be worth their time*  
      *\- Stop the Clickbait bullshit, its keeping you stuck looking like the masses*   
      *\- Try giving real value, folks love and appreciate that      shit*  
      *\- Your video/post should Entertain, Educate,Motivate, Engage or*     
         *Evoke Emotions. (Or mix of all these biches )*   
*6️⃣ Your audiences are real people, treat em like it*  
     *\- The folks on your socials are just like you, human. Take a*     
         *couple minutes to reply, message and*  
         *Encourage when you get the time*    
     *\- You want folks to follow you and do business with you?*   
*Make sure you give them good reasons to do so*   
*7️⃣ Expand your reach and grow your brand*  
      *\- Following these steps laid out will build confidence and allow you to be comfortable*   
        *Creating content on all social media platforms*  
      *\- Once you’ve found your groove and tested different content styles you can then start*  
        *Repurposing your content to other places*    
*(do this sooner if you have the bandwidth)*  
*8️⃣ Rinse and repeat these steps for more than 7 days and you will grow in every area*  
*For real though, you need to be consistent more than anything else*  
*Oh and This is the recipe of how I’ve grown my audience to over 60,000 folks and A small percentage of them have become customers and clients generating \$1M+ in sales The last 2 years*  
*So take this shit serious if you really want to grow your Personal Brand and make bank*   
*And when the time is right we can chat about doing this together with weekly coaching and support so you can do this much faster without the complicated tech, head spinning software, brain hurting funnels and still start building a business that you love while dwarfing any income you've made at a job*

*I picked the girls up from school today and I heard Miranda say something that made me ask her to repeat it.*  
*She said....*  
*'Daddy & Mommy are rich'*  
*I asked why she thought that*  
*Her response, 'because you have a lot of money daddy'*  
*I had to correct her real quick*   
*I told her that we weren't rich, and that we didn't have a lot of money lol.*  
*I told her that we are very wealthy though*  
*'That's the same as rich ain't it?'*  
*Kinda*   
*We aren't rich with money, but...*  
*I told her I felt wealthy because I have you two girls and your mommy.*  
*Yalls love is priceless.*  
*That got two big ole grins out of them.*  
*"Family isn't just an important thing, it's everything\!"*  
*Super grateful to have these 3 ladies in my life*  
*Also to have a few close friends that are really just extended family.*  
*I don't have to be rich later, because I'm wealthy now.*  
*Everything else is just life's bonuses*  
*Make sure ya know what's important to you and don't let that shit slip away from ya.*  
*Keep building my friends, I'm rooting for yall\!\!*

*My wife is packing her shit and leaving me today*  
*I don't feel good about it, at all but......*  
*.*  
*.*  
*.*  
*.*  
*.*  
*She'll be back Monday lol.*  
*She's going on a well deserved girls trip to the beach*  
*It'll be just me and the girls all weekend wrecking havoc taking part in daddy daughter Shenanigans and shit.*  
*I love my wife and I'm super thankful for the relationship we have.*  
*Our girls see us loving each other everyday, something I didn't see as a kid*  
*Brooke has always been by my side with all my crazy ideas.*  
*She  doesn't question my business decisions, only supports because she knows where my decisions stem from. (Her & our girls)*  
*I wrote her a note about a year or so ago, we still have it and it holds a great reminder / lesson. I'll add it to this post when we get home.*  
*I hope ya have a great time honey, you deserve it as a super mom and bomb ass wife.*  
*P.S. I'll be sure to push all your buttons before ya go, because ya know, I'm really good at it lol.*  
*I'll catch yall on the Flipside*

*Got word that a client has done almost \$90k in their business the last 11ish months with our unique approach to business as a whole*  
*The most amazing part to me is that they use to make only \$200 a month, or \$2400 a year in their part of the world.*  
*The model they used to grow in a healthy way is the same one we're talking about today at 11am est inside my humble abode (FYS Community)*  
*We're running a breakdown of what we're putting inside of GJ 2.0, it shall be Juicy (pun intended)*  
*Our Curriculum and Model of growing businesses has already been voted top notch based on Culture, Community and Results.*  
*Also severely underpriced according to Value & Support, hence the price increase coming soon.*  
*After having a catch up sesh with our guy JC I had a nice 💡 moment, multiple actually.*  
*The way things have been going, all trending upwards, it's pretty wild from a reflection standpoint looking at all that has changed.*  
*I know I need to show up more and share all the growing pains that have caused an insane lifestyle change.*  
*I have content in a few places and with new operation SOPs in place thst will be turned up a bit.*  
*But the best place to be will remain my FB group.*  
*There's no judgment there and it's my house, I get to share what I want and how I want with the sole goal of being of the highest value to those inside.*  
*If you'd like to be an active member there and catch all the upcoming changes and insights, just let me know and I'll get ya in there.*  
*Live at 11am est today covering GJ 2.0 and all that it entails.*  
*I hope yall have a kickass day and I'll catch yall on the Flipside*

*Finished out the girls Spring Break with a few days of fun, great peeps, and good eats\!*  
*They're back to school amd we're back to our not so normal schedule (by design)*  
*Had an absolutely killer 4 day Weekend hitting Kings Dominion with the Grandaddy and GiGi followed with a day at the lake with the besties.*  
*Today was pretty laid back but still getting some work done.*  
*Every Monday Nate and I catch up for our weekly planning and quarterly assessment on projects*  
*Ya See GJ 2.0 is in the works and is scheduled to relaunch June 26th following a 50% price increase*  
*We're knocking on the door of 500 members in GJ that follow our model that eliminates 90% of the tedious tasks that organic marketers Hate.*  
*With what we created We've helped over 1400 customers and clients with all kinds of different businesses*   
*And the Juice is about to get sweeter.*  
*This Wednesday Flate is going live in my private community to Jive on everything we're adding to GJ 2.0*  
*New insights*   
*New strategies*   
*Leveraged income*  
*There's a little secret we've learned and hardly anyone has picked up on it.*  
*It has something to do with not needing new customers and clients every month*  
*I had a pretty awesome realization on Saturday*   
*I almost felt guilty for enjoying myself*  
*Actually I did for a bit, then reset.*  
*I mentioned this to our Core Clients too*  
*Self doubt and ongoing challenges are a sure sign of Growth, new levels, different phases and seasons.*  
*Since the 'past me' set some goals in stone based on 'future me' I'm now where 'past me' wanted to be.*  
*Problems are different*   
*Business is different*   
*Life is different*   
*Work is at a minimum*   
*Family time is at maximum*   
*Just how I like it*  
*I 'get' to enjoy a schedule and level of freedom that most never will experience due to accepting the masses path.*  
*It took some time and I'm glad I stayed the course because there's lots more I'm going to do...*  
*...in time & how I want without sacrificing what's most important.*  
*So keep your eyes peeled, we're cooking up some more cool stuff that's gunna help a lot of folks with GJ 2.0*  
*Let me know with a GIF if ya need an invite for Wednesday at 11am est*  
*We're off to go grab some good grub from the Mexican spot\!*  
*I'll swing over to my group after and drop some sauce with a sneak peek of the what's to come.*  
*I'll catch yall on the Flipside*

*Do ya see all the love in this photo?*  
*I was finishing up a quick email about hangout sesh happening today and as I was about to close my laptop I realized something.....*  
*I have an absolutely great relationship with my wife*  
*And we have a damn good relationship with our kids*  
*The last few years Brooke & I haven't missed a thing with either them.*  
*We've made it to every school event & participated in all the fundraisers and what not.*  
*We stay to ourself for the most part and have a very small group of friends that we'd do anything for and vice versa.*  
*We've got to the age that drama isn't invited and peace of mind is a non negotiable lol*  
*We've got short term & long term goals set*  
*Setting up a concrete investment fund for ourselves and the girls so they are set as they ease into adulthood.*  
*Recently enrolled our family of 4 into new solid insurance with health, dental & vision and great benefits being self employed.*  
*We don't have everything figured out by any means but the good lord knows my ass is absolutely full of gratitude for what I have and what I've come from.*  
*The business I dreamed of having back in 2017 has now become a reality*   
*Though a large majority of folks laughed and didn't believe in what I wanted to do, I kept going.*  
*Yes, it was hard*  
*Yes, it took a lot of work & investing time & \$*  
*Yes, it was challenging, and still very much so*  
*Our problems are different*   
*The stress is different*   
*But in a sense, it's controlled problems & stress*  
*You know the saying....*  
*Being broke is hard*  
*Being wealthy is hard*  
*Choose your hard*  
*To be clear my country bumkin ass isn't wealthy with money, yet\!*  
*But I'm without a doubt rich with the family and friends I have, paired with the schedule I've worked hard for.*  
*I get the pleasure of working with some amazing individuals*   
*I get to keep learning the things I do NOT know and in return teaching the very things I DO know so that others can move faster in a healthy way.*  
*Entrepreneurship isn't for everyone but you know when you're eat up with it, and those around you can tell too*  
*Not everyone will understand you and the vision you have, they're not suppose to.*  
*But there will be folks that align with you and your vision and yall will do amazing things together.*  
*Just know that if you think big and have big ideas you'll raise the eyebrows of many people*  
*Some will throw shade*   
*Some will support you 1,000%*  
*The shade throwers will likely be close in proximity & become strangers over time*  
*The supporters will likely be complete strangers & become close in proximity over time*  
*Stay the course*  
*Keep rocking with that purpose that tickles your brain each day, almost as it's your calling*  
*Because it is*  
*This is just a few thoughts running through my ADHD brain as I sip my coffee on the front porch listening to my kids roller skate through the house.*  
*I've got 2 calls today*  
*1 with some OG clients that we've helped do some amazing things like quit their jobs and build a business of impact & profit.*  
*1 with some private clients that are making waves in their own respective market place, mapping out some new stuff to end Q2 with a bang & set themselves up for Q3&4*  
*Then we'll likely end the day with the strawberry patch and see both sets of grandparents.*  
*I hope this gets ya gears turning and ya have a kickass day.*  
*I'll catch yall on the Flipside 😎*

*I didn't start out to become a mentor or a coach in the realm of business & consulting, it just happened naturally after years of failure, experience and results.*  
*I got this nice little desk plaque paired with a card in the mail the other day and I gotta say it pulled on my heart strings quite a bit.*  
*Recieved it from a private client who's done some amazing things in her business, especially starting from ground zero.*  
*Jamie, I highly appreciate this and it has really made my days better.*  
*I say that because some days I question if Im having a positive impact on folks*  
*Or if I'm worthy of the success & influence I have from what I've created.*  
*Then I get messages from students and clients that bring it all home to show me that I, in fact, do have a positive impact on folks.*  
*And I am worthy of the success & influence.*  
*Know that I don't take it lightly at all.*  
*I carry that shit proudly and do my best to accept the awesome comments and testimonials from those learning from me.*  
*Truth is, I'm just a regular guy in a small town that has worked longer, failed more and just never gave up.*  
*Getting this gift in the mail, thst now lives on my desk as a reminder, is one of the best things I've ever recieved.*  
*It beats every monetary win I've experienced by a long shot.*  
*Mainly because it shows an appreciation between two humans.*  
*I'm in the people business, I care about the people we work with and want to see them win\!*  
*The saying I'm known for applys big time here....*  
*"Relationships over Revenue"*  
*Thank you again Jamie, means the world to me.*  
*I believe we're gunna get the girls from school early, go grab some 2" Ribeye steaks from Spiveys and cook on the grill this evening.*  
*I hope you all have a kickass Weekend & I'll catch yall on the Flipside*
